<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲低资源语言的数据稀缺限制了NLP发展，需要评估不同数据增强方法在真实低资源场景下的有效性

Method: 使用两种数据增强方法：1) LLM生成（Gemini 2.5 Flash），2) 回译（NLLB-200）；在豪萨语和丰语上评估，使用MasakhaNER 2.0和MasakhaPOS基准测试命名实体识别和词性标注任务

Result: 增强效果取决于任务类型：NER任务中两种方法均无改进（豪萨语下降0.24% F1，丰语下降1.81% F1）；词性标注中LLM增强提升丰语0.33%准确率，回译提升豪萨语0.17%；同一LLM生成数据在不同任务中产生相反效果

Conclusion: LLM生成质量不能预测增强成功，数据增强应被视为任务特定的干预措施而非普遍有益的预处理步骤

Abstract: Data scarcity limits NLP development for low-resource African languages. We evaluate two data augmentation methods -- LLM-based generation (Gemini 2.5 Flash) and back-translation (NLLB-200) -- for Hausa and Fongbe, two West African languages that differ substantially in LLM generation quality. We assess augmentation on named entity recognition (NER) and part-of-speech (POS) tagging using MasakhaNER 2.0 and MasakhaPOS benchmarks. Our results reveal that augmentation effectiveness depends on task type rather than language or LLM quality alone. For NER, neither method improves over baseline for either language; LLM augmentation reduces Hausa NER by 0.24% F1 and Fongbe NER by 1.81% F1. For POS tagging, LLM augmentation improves Fongbe by 0.33% accuracy, while back-translation improves Hausa by 0.17%; back-translation reduces Fongbe POS by 0.35% and has negligible effect on Hausa POS. The same LLM-generated synthetic data produces opposite effects across tasks for Fongbe -- hurting NER while helping POS -- suggesting task structure governs augmentation outcomes more than synthetic data quality. These findings challenge the assumption that LLM generation quality predicts augmentation success, and provide actionable guidance: data augmentation should be treated as a task-specific intervention rather than a universally beneficial preprocessing step.

</details>


### [39] [Transforming External Knowledge into Triplets for Enhanced Retrieval in RAG of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12610)
*Xudong Wang,Chaoning Zhang,Qigan Sun,Zhenzhen Huang,Chang Lu,Sheng Zheng,Zeyu Ma,Caiyan Qin,Yang Yang,Hengtao Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tri-RAG：基于结构化三元组的检索增强生成框架，通过将知识转换为条件-证明-结论三元组，提升检索效率和生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG方法检索和拼接非结构化文本片段作为上下文，常引入冗余或弱相关信息，导致上下文过度积累、语义对齐降低和推理链碎片化，从而降低生成质量并增加token消耗

Method: 提出Tri-RAG框架，将外部知识从自然语言自动转换为标准化的结构化三元组（条件、证明、结论），使用轻量级提示适配捕获知识片段间的逻辑关系；以三元组的"条件"部分作为显式语义锚点进行检索匹配，避免直接拼接冗长原始文本

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，Tri-RAG显著提高了检索质量和推理效率，在复杂推理场景中产生更稳定的生成行为和更高效的资源利用

Conclusion: Tri-RAG通过结构化三元组表示实现了检索准确性和上下文token效率之间的良好平衡，为RAG系统提供了一种更有效的知识组织和检索方法

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates hallucination in large language models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge during generation. However, the effectiveness of RAG depends not only on the design of the retriever and the capacity of the underlying model, but also on how retrieved evidence is structured and aligned with the query. Existing RAG approaches typically retrieve and concatenate unstructured text fragments as context, which often introduces redundant or weakly relevant information. This practice leads to excessive context accumulation, reduced semantic alignment, and fragmented reasoning chains, thereby degrading generation quality while increasing token consumption. To address these challenges, we propose Tri-RAG, a structured triplet-based retrieval framework that improves retrieval efficiency through reasoning-aligned context construction. Tri-RAG automatically transforms external knowledge from natural language into standardized structured triplets consisting of Condition, Proof, and Conclusion, explicitly capturing logical relations among knowledge fragments using lightweight prompt-based adaptation with frozen model parameters. Building on this representation, the triplet head Condition is treated as an explicit semantic anchor for retrieval and matching, enabling precise identification of query-relevant knowledge units without directly concatenating lengthy raw texts. As a result, Tri-RAG achieves a favorable balance between retrieval accuracy and context token efficiency. Experimental results across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that Tri-RAG significantly improves retrieval quality and reasoning efficiency, while producing more stable generation behavior and more efficient resource utilization in complex reasoning scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [Multilingual Multi-Label Emotion Classification at Scale with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12633)
*Vadim Borisov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建大规模多语言多标签情感分类数据集，训练并评估6个多语言Transformer模型，XLM-R-Large在23种语言上表现优异，超越英语专用模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言情感分类面临标注数据稀缺的问题：现有语料库主要是英语、单标签且覆盖语言有限。需要解决这一差距，支持更广泛的语言和更复杂的情感分类任务。

Method: 1. 构建包含23种语言、超过100万多标签样本的大规模合成训练语料库（每种语言5万样本），覆盖11种情感类别，采用文化适应生成和程序化质量过滤；2. 在相同条件下训练和比较6个多语言Transformer编码器，从DistilBERT（1.35亿参数）到XLM-R-Large（5.6亿参数）；3. 使用领域内测试集和零样本评估（GoEmotions和SemEval-2018 Task 1 E-c）验证模型性能。

Result: 1. 在领域内测试集上，XLM-R-Large达到0.868 F1-micro和0.987 AUC-micro；2. 在零样本评估中，XLM-R-Large在阈值无关的排序指标上匹配或超越英语专用模型：AP-micro（0.636）和LRAP（0.804）持平，AUC-micro（0.810 vs. 0.787）超越；3. 模型原生支持所有23种语言，最佳基础规模模型已公开。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模多语言多标签情感分类数据集和系统评估多语言Transformer模型，证明了合成数据生成和程序化质量过滤的有效性。XLM-R-Large在多语言情感分类任务中表现出色，甚至超越英语专用模型，为多语言情感分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Emotion classification in multilingual settings remains constrained by the scarcity of annotated data: existing corpora are predominantly English, single-label, and cover few languages. We address this gap by constructing a large-scale synthetic training corpus of over 1M multi-label samples (50k per language) across 23 languages: Arabic, Bengali, Dutch, English, French, German, Hindi, Indonesian, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Mandarin, Polish, Portuguese, Punjabi, Russian, Spanish, Swahili, Tamil, Turkish, Ukrainian, Urdu, and Vietnamese, covering 11 emotion categories using culturally-adapted generation and programmatic quality filtering. We train and compare six multilingual transformer encoders, from DistilBERT (135M parameters) to XLM-R-Large (560M parameters), under identical conditions. On our in-domain test set, XLM-R-Large achieves 0.868 F1-micro and 0.987 AUC-micro. To validate against human-annotated data, we evaluate all models zero-shot on GoEmotions (English) and SemEval-2018 Task 1 E-c (English, Arabic, Spanish). On threshold-free ranking metrics, XLM-R-Large matches or exceeds English-only specialist models, tying on AP-micro (0.636) and LRAP (0.804) while surpassing on AUC-micro (0.810 vs. 0.787), while natively supporting all 23 languages. The best base-sized model is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/tabularisai/multilingual-emotion-classification

</details>


### [41] [Learning Chain Of Thoughts Prompts for Predicting Entities, Relations, and even Literals on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12651)
*Alkid Baci,Luke Friedrichs,Caglar Demir,N'Dah Jean Kouagou,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RALP将知识图谱链接预测重构为提示学习问题，使用贝叶斯优化从少量训练样本中学习字符串形式的思维链提示作为三元组评分函数，显著提升KGE模型性能并增强对未见实体、关系和字面值的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识图谱嵌入模型在链接预测上表现良好，但难以处理未见实体、关系和字面值，限制了其在动态异构图中的应用。相比之下，预训练大语言模型通过提示能有效泛化，因此探索基于提示的LLM推理作为嵌入方法的灵活替代方案。

Method: RALP将链接预测重构为提示学习问题，学习字符串形式的思维链提示作为三元组评分函数。采用MIPRO算法进行贝叶斯优化，仅需少于30个训练样本即可找到有效提示，无需梯度访问。推理时预测缺失的实体、关系或完整三元组，并根据学习到的提示分配置信度分数。

Result: 在转导、数值和OWL实例检索基准测试中，RALP将最先进KGE模型的MRR提升了超过5%。在具有复杂类表达式的OWL推理任务中（如∃hasChild.Female, ≥5 hasChild.Female），获得了超过88%的Jaccard相似度。通过高质量推断的三元组增强了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于提示的LLM推理是嵌入方法的灵活替代方案，RALP通过少量样本学习有效提示，显著提升KGE性能并增强对未见元素的泛化能力。该方法为动态异构图中的链接预测提供了新思路，代码已开源。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models perform well on link prediction but struggle with unseen entities, relations, and especially literals, limiting their use in dynamic, heterogeneous graphs. In contrast, pretrained large language models (LLMs) generalize effectively through prompting. We reformulate link prediction as a prompt learning problem and introduce RALP, which learns string-based chain-of-thought (CoT) prompts as scoring functions for triples. Using Bayesian Optimization through MIPRO algorithm, RALP identifies effective prompts from fewer than 30 training examples without gradient access. At inference, RALP predicts missing entities, relations or whole triples and assigns confidence scores based on the learned prompt. We evaluate on transductive, numerical, and OWL instance retrieval benchmarks. RALP improves state-of-the-art KGE models by over 5% MRR across datasets and enhances generalization via high-quality inferred triples. On OWL reasoning tasks with complex class expressions (e.g., $\exists hasChild.Female$, $\geq 5 \; hasChild.Female$), it achieves over 88% Jaccard similarity. These results highlight prompt-based LLM reasoning as a flexible alternative to embedding-based methods. We release our implementation, training, and evaluation pipeline as open source: https://github.com/dice-group/RALP .

</details>


### [42] [InsightFlow: LLM-Driven Synthesis of Patient Narratives for Mental Health into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12721)
*Shreya Gupta,Prottay Kumar Adhikary,Bhavyaa Dave,Salam Michael Singh,Aniket Deroy,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InsightFlow：基于LLM的自动5P因果图生成系统，从心理治疗对话中构建临床个案概念化图表，评估显示其与专家标注具有可比性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床个案概念化（5P框架）需要将患者症状和心理社会因素组织成因果模型，但传统方法耗时且存在临床医生间差异。需要自动化工具来辅助这一过程，提高效率和一致性。

Method: 开发InsightFlow系统，使用大型语言模型（LLM）从患者-治疗师对话中自动生成符合5P框架的因果图。使用46个心理治疗初始访谈转录本（由临床专家标注）进行评估，采用结构相似性（NetSimile）、语义相似性（嵌入向量）和专家评分三种评估方法。

Result: LLM生成的图表在结构相似性上与标注者间一致性相当，语义对齐度高。专家评估认为输出具有中等完整性、一致性和临床实用性。LLM图表倾向于形成更互连的结构（相比人类图表的链式模式），但整体复杂性和内容覆盖度相似。

Conclusion: LLM能够生成在专家实践自然变异范围内的临床有意义的个案概念化图表。InsightFlow展示了自动因果建模增强临床工作流程的潜力，未来需要改进时间推理和减少冗余。

Abstract: Clinical case formulation organizes patient symptoms and psychosocial factors into causal models, often using the 5P framework. However, constructing such graphs from therapy transcripts is time consuming and varies across clinicians. We present InsightFlow, an LLM based approach that automatically generates 5P aligned causal graphs from patient-therapist dialogues. Using 46 psychotherapy intake transcripts annotated by clinical experts, we evaluate LLM generated graphs against human formulations using structural (NetSimile), semantic (embedding similarity), and expert rated clinical criteria. The generated graphs show structural similarity comparable to inter annotator agreement and high semantic alignment with human graphs. Expert evaluations rate the outputs as moderately complete, consistent, and clinically useful. While LLM graphs tend to form more interconnected structures compared to the chain like patterns of human graphs, overall complexity and content coverage are similar. These results suggest that LLMs can produce clinically meaningful case formulation graphs within the natural variability of expert practice. InsightFlow highlights the potential of automated causal modeling to augment clinical workflows, with future work needed to improve temporal reasoning and reduce redundancy.

</details>


### [43] [Token-Level Policy Optimization: Linking Group-Level Rewards to Token-Level Aggregation via Sequence-Level Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12736)
*Xingyu Lin,Yilin Wen,Du Su,Jinchang Hou,En Wang,Wenbin Liu,Chenfu Bao,Zhonghou Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEPO是一种新颖的令牌级优化框架，通过序列级似然和KL散度掩码约束解决GRPO等方法在链式思维推理中面临的令牌级稀疏奖励问题，显著提升数学推理性能并增强训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等熵正则化方法在处理链式思维推理中的令牌级稀疏奖励时存在困难，容易导致熵崩溃或模型退化。现有方法依赖未分化的令牌级熵正则化，在稀疏令牌奖励下效果不佳。

Method: 提出TEPO框架：1) 利用序列级似然通过令牌级聚合将组级奖励与单个令牌关联；2) 引入针对具有正优势和熵减少的令牌的令牌级KL散度掩码约束，以缓解策略的突然更新。

Result: TEPO在数学推理基准测试中达到最先进性能，同时显著增强训练稳定性，与GRPO/DAPO相比收敛时间减少50%。

Conclusion: TEPO通过创新的令牌级优化机制有效解决了链式思维推理中的稀疏奖励问题，为大型语言模型的推理能力提升提供了更稳定高效的训练方法。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has significantly advanced the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), particularly in their mathemat ical reasoning performance. However, GRPO and related entropy regularization methods still struggle with token-level sparse-rewards, which is an inherent chal lenge in chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. These approaches often rely on undifferen tiated token-level entropy regularization, which easily leads to entropy collapse or model degradation under sparse token rewards. In this work, we propose TEPO, a novel token-level framework that (1) leverages sequence-level likelihood to link group-level rewards with individual tokens via token-level aggregation, and (2) introduces a token-level KL-Divergence mask constraint that targets tokens with positive advantages and decreasing entropy to mitigate abrupt policy updates. Experiments demonstrate that TEPO not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on mathematical reasoning benchmarks but also markedly enhances training stability, reducing convergence time by 50% compared with GRPO/DAPO.

</details>


### [44] [Universal NER v2: Towards a Massively Multilingual Named Entity Recognition Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12744)
*Terra Blevins,Stephen Mayhew,Marek Šuppa,Hila Gonen,Shachar Mirkin,Vasile Pais,Kaja Dobrovoljc,Voula Giouli,Jun Kevin,Eugene Jang,Eungseo Kim,Jeongyeon Seo,Xenophon Gialis,Yuval Pinter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UNER项目致力于构建多语言命名实体识别的黄金标准基准数据集，使用通用标签集和详细标注指南来收集标准化的跨语言命名实体标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言语言模型承诺将LLM的优势带给多种语言的使用者，但大多数语言中用于验证这些假设的黄金标准评估基准仍然稀缺，需要构建标准化的多语言NER基准数据集。

Method: 项目采用通用标签集和详尽的标注指南，收集标准化的跨语言命名实体标注，受到其他核心NLP任务的大规模多语言努力（如Universal Dependencies）的启发。

Result: 第一版UNER v1于2024年发布，项目持续扩展，拥有活跃社区中的各种组织者、标注者和合作者。

Conclusion: UNER项目正在构建标准化的多语言NER基准数据集，以解决多语言评估基准稀缺的问题，促进多语言语言模型的公平评估。

Abstract: While multilingual language models promise to bring the benefits of LLMs to speakers of many languages, gold-standard evaluation benchmarks in most languages to interrogate these assumptions remain scarce. The Universal NER project, now entering its fourth year, is dedicated to building gold-standard multilingual Named Entity Recognition (NER) benchmark datasets. Inspired by existing massively multilingual efforts for other core NLP tasks (e.g., Universal Dependencies), the project uses a general tagset and thorough annotation guidelines to collect standardized, cross-lingual annotations of named entity spans. The first installment (UNER v1) was released in 2024, and the project has continued and expanded since then, with various organizers, annotators, and collaborators in an active community.

</details>


### [45] [Generating Effective CoT Traces for Mitigating Causal Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12748)
*Yiheng Zhao,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对小规模语言模型在事件因果关系识别中的因果幻觉问题，提出通过生成符合特定标准的思维链轨迹进行微调的方法，并引入因果幻觉率作为量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在复杂推理任务中表现出色，但在事件因果关系识别中存在严重的因果幻觉问题，特别是在参数规模≤1.5B的小模型中。目前缺乏专门针对ECI的思维链轨迹数据集，也没有量化因果幻觉的指标。

Method: 首先研究有效思维链轨迹应具备的标准以缓解小模型的因果幻觉；然后设计生成符合这些标准的思维链轨迹的流程；同时引入新的量化指标——因果幻觉率来指导标准制定和验证流程有效性。

Result: 使用本文流程生成的思维链轨迹进行微调，不仅显著减少了小规模LLM的因果幻觉，还提高了平均准确率。微调后的模型展现出强大的跨数据集、跨难度泛化能力，以及对误导性干预提示的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的思维链轨迹生成流程和因果幻觉率量化指标，能够有效解决小规模语言模型在事件因果关系识别中的因果幻觉问题，提升模型性能并增强泛化能力。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) excel in complex reasoning tasks, they suffer from severe causal hallucination in event causality identification (ECI), particularly in smaller models ($\leq$1.5B parameters). A promising approach to address this issue is to fine-tune them with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. However, there is currently a lack of CoT trace dataset available for ECI. In this paper, we first investigate the essential criteria that effective CoT traces should possess to mitigate causal hallucination in smaller models. We then design a pipeline to generate CoT traces that meet these criteria. Moreover, since there is currently no metric for quantifying causal hallucination, we also introduce a new metric, the Causal Hallucination Rate (CHR), to quantify causal hallucination, guide the formulation of effective CoT trace criteria, and validate the effectiveness of our pipeline. Our experiments show that fine-tuning with the CoT traces generated by our pipeline not only substantially reduces causal hallucination in smaller LLMs but also improves mean accuracy. Moreover, the fine-tuned models exhibit strong cross-dataset and cross-difficulty generalization, as well as robustness under misleading intervention prompts.

</details>


### [46] [Teaching LLMs Human-Like Editing of Inappropriate Argumentation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12770)
*Timon Ziegenbein,Maja Stahl,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种强化学习方法，教导大语言模型进行类人编辑，通过自包含的句子级编辑建议提升论证的适当性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现人类与LLM在文本编辑策略上存在显著差异：LLM倾向于进行多个分散的编辑且常改变原意，而人类则更倾向于将相关修改封装在自包含、保持原意的编辑中。这种差异影响了LLM编辑文本的适当性和可接受性。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，使用群体相对策略优化（group relative policy optimization）训练LLM。构建多组件奖励函数，联合优化编辑级语义相似性、流畅性、模式一致性以及论证级适当性。该方法生成自包含的句子级编辑建议，可独立接受或拒绝。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，该方法优于竞争基线和现有最先进的类人编辑方法。多轮编辑能达到接近完全重写的适当性水平。

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习方法能有效教导LLM进行类人编辑，生成自包含、保持原意的编辑建议，显著提升论证的适当性，使LLM编辑更接近人类编辑策略。

Abstract: Editing human-written text has become a standard use case of large language models (LLMs), for example, to make one's arguments more appropriate for a discussion. Comparing human to LLM-generated edits, however, we observe a mismatch in editing strategies: While LLMs often perform multiple scattered edits and tend to change meaning notably, humans rather encapsulate dependent changes in self-contained, meaning-preserving edits. In this paper, we present a reinforcement learning approach that teaches LLMs human-like editing to improve the appropriateness of arguments. Our approach produces self-contained sentence-level edit suggestions that can be accepted or rejected independently. We train the approach using group relative policy optimization with a multi-component reward function that jointly optimizes edit-level semantic similarity, fluency, and pattern conformity as well as argument-level appropriateness. In automatic and human evaluation, it outperforms competitive baselines and the state of the art in human-like editing, with multi-round editing achieving appropriateness close to full rewriting.

</details>


### [47] [EvoSpark: Endogenous Interactive Agent Societies for Unified Long-Horizon Narrative Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12776)
*Shiyu He,Minchi Kuang,Mengxian Wang,Bin Hu,Tingxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoSpark框架解决LLM多智能体系统中内生叙事演化的随机性问题，通过分层叙事记忆和生成场景机制确保长时程叙事的逻辑一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中内生叙事演化面临生成涌现的随机性问题，特别是长时程模拟中存在社会记忆堆叠（冲突关系状态累积无解）和叙事空间失调（空间逻辑与情节脱节）的挑战

Method: 提出EvoSpark框架，包含：1) 分层叙事记忆使用角色社会演化基础作为动态认知，代谢经验解决历史冲突；2) 生成场景机制强制角色-位置-情节对齐；3) 统一叙事操作引擎整合涌现角色锚定协议，将随机触发转化为持久角色

Result: 实验表明EvoSpark在不同范式上显著优于基线方法，能够持续生成表达性强且连贯的叙事体验

Conclusion: EvoSpark为内生交互智能体社会提供了维持逻辑连贯长时程叙事的有效框架，将最小前提扩展为开放演化的故事世界

Abstract: Realizing endogenous narrative evolution in LLM-based multi-agent systems is hindered by the inherent stochasticity of generative emergence. In particular, long-horizon simulations suffer from social memory stacking, where conflicting relational states accumulate without resolution, and narrative-spatial dissonance, where spatial logic detaches from the evolving plot. To bridge this gap, we propose EvoSpark, a framework specifically designed to sustain logically coherent long-horizon narratives within Endogenous Interactive Agent Societies. To ensure consistency, the Stratified Narrative Memory employs a Role Socio-Evolutionary Base as living cognition, dynamically metabolizing experiences to resolve historical conflicts. Complementarily, Generative Mise-en-Scène mechanism enforces Role-Location-Plot alignment, synchronizing character presence with the narrative flow. Underpinning these is the Unified Narrative Operation Engine, which integrates an Emergent Character Grounding Protocol to transform stochastic sparking into persistent characters. This engine establishes a substrate that expands a minimal premise into an open-ended, evolving story world. Experiments demonstrate that EvoSpark significantly outperforms baselines across diverse paradigms, enabling the sustained generation of expressive and coherent narrative experiences.

</details>


### [48] [Growing Pains: Extensible and Efficient LLM Benchmarking Via Fixed Parameter Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12843)
*Eliya Habba,Itay Itzhak,Asaf Yehudai,Yotam Perlitz,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen,Gabriel Stanovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于多维项目反应理论(IRT)的框架，使用锚定项目将新基准校准到评估套件中，保持已校准项目参数固定，实现随时间扩展基准套件的同时保持分数可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型和基准测试的快速发布使得评估每个模型在每个数据集上的成本越来越高。实践中，模型通常在不同样本上评估，导致跨研究分数难以比较。需要一种方法支持数据集随时间引入的现实评估场景。

Method: 基于多维项目反应理论(IRT)的框架，使用锚定项目校准新基准到评估套件，保持先前校准的项目参数固定。每个数据集使用固定锚定集，使不同评估时期的结果可直接比较。

Result: 在超过400个模型的大规模实验中，该框架仅使用每个数据集100个锚定问题即可将全评估性能预测在2-3个百分点内，Spearman ρ≥0.9用于排名保持，表明可以在恒定评估成本下随时间扩展基准套件。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够随时间扩展基准套件同时保持分数可比性，每个新数据集的评估成本恒定，解决了模型评估中跨研究分数比较的难题。

Abstract: The rapid release of both language models and benchmarks makes it increasingly costly to evaluate every model on every dataset. In practice, models are often evaluated on different samples, making scores difficult to compare across studies. To address this, we propose a framework based on multidimensional Item Response Theory (IRT) that uses anchor items to calibrate new benchmarks to the evaluation suite while holding previously calibrated item parameters fixed. Our approach supports a realistic evaluation setting in which datasets are introduced over time and models are evaluated only on the datasets available at the time of evaluation, while a fixed anchor set for each dataset is used so that results from different evaluation periods can be compared directly. In large-scale experiments on more than $400$ models, our framework predicts full-evaluation performance within 2-3 percentage points using only $100$ anchor questions per dataset, with Spearman $ρ\geq 0.9$ for ranking preservation, showing that it is possible to extend benchmark suites over time while preserving score comparability, at a constant evaluation cost per new dataset. Code available at https://github.com/eliyahabba/growing-pains

</details>


### [49] [Round-Trip Translation Reveals What Frontier Multilingual Benchmarks Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12911)
*Ronald Skorobogat,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出当前多语言基准测试主要衡量数学推理和事实回忆能力而非多语言熟练度，提出使用往返翻译作为替代评估方法，并引入LiT基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿模型的多语言评估主要基于类似推理和知识基准的结构，但这些基准实际上测量的是数学推理和事实回忆能力，而非真正的多语言熟练度，导致评估结果与现实世界多语言任务表现不一致

Method: 提出往返翻译评估方法：将源语言文本翻译到目标语言再翻译回源语言，通过比较原始文本与返回文本的语义差异来暴露多语言生成能力的缺陷；同时引入Lost in Translation (LiT)基准，涵盖全球广泛使用的语言

Result: 往返翻译评估与LMArena用户评分的相关性高达ρ=0.94，无需人工参考翻译，且不需要比被测模型更强大的多语言评判者；LiT基准为多语言前沿模型提供了现实评估

Conclusion: 往返翻译是评估多语言能力的有效替代方法，能够更准确地反映模型在实际多语言任务中的表现，LiT基准为多语言前沿模型的现实评估提供了挑战性测试平台

Abstract: Multilingual benchmarks guide the development of frontier models. Yet multilingual evaluations reported by frontier models are structured similar to popular reasoning and knowledge benchmarks, but across many languages. We show such benchmarks, and consequently multilingual evaluations, measure mathematical reasoning and factual recall, not multilingual proficiency. For example, thinking variants dramatically outperform instruct variants on these benchmarks, yet often perform worse on real-world multilingual tasks, such as LMArena. We propose a simple alternative: evaluate multilingual capability via round-trip translation. Given text in a source language, translate it to a target language and back; semantic gaps between the original and result expose failures in multilingual generation capabilities. Round-trip translation correlates almost perfectly (\r{ho} = 0.94) with user ratings on LMArena with our benchmark, requires no human reference translations, and does not require a more capable multilingual judge than tested models. Lastly, we introduce Lost in Translation (LiT), a challenging round-trip translation benchmark spanning widely spoken languages worldwide, for realistic evaluation of multilingual frontier models.

</details>


### [50] [MetFuse: Figurative Fusion between Metonymy and Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12919)
*Saptarshi Ghosh,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MetFuse框架和数据集，用于研究转喻和隐喻在自然语言中的融合现象，包含1000个人工验证的四元组句子，实验表明混合示例能提升两种修辞的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转喻和隐喻在自然语言中经常同时出现，但以往的计算研究大多将它们孤立研究。需要建立一个框架来系统研究这两种修辞的融合现象及其相互影响。

Method: 提出了一个框架，将字面句子转化为三种修辞变体：转喻、隐喻和混合。基于此构建了MetFuse数据集，包含1000个人工验证的四元组（字面句+三种修辞变体）。在八个现有基准上进行了外部实验，分析数据增强效果。

Result: 使用MetFuse数据增强训练能一致提升转喻和隐喻分类性能，混合示例对转喻任务的提升最大。人类标注者和大型语言模型在混合句子中比在纯转喻句子中能更好地识别转喻，表明隐喻的存在使转喻名词更加显式。

Conclusion: 转喻和隐喻的融合研究对自然语言处理具有重要意义，MetFuse数据集为这一领域提供了首个专用资源。隐喻的存在有助于转喻识别，表明两种修辞在认知处理中存在相互作用。

Abstract: Metonymy and metaphor often co-occur in natural language, yet computational work has studied them largely in isolation. We introduce a framework that transforms a literal sentence into three figurative variants: metonymic, metaphoric, and hybrid. Using this framework, we construct MetFuse, the first dedicated dataset of figurative fusion between metonymy and metaphor, containing 1,000 human-verified meaning-aligned quadruplets totaling 4,000 sentences. Extrinsic experiments on eight existing benchmarks show that augmenting training data with MetFuse consistently improves both metonymy and metaphor classification, with hybrid examples yielding the largest gains on metonymy tasks. Using this dataset, we also analyze how the presence of one figurative type impacts another. Our findings show that both human annotators and large language models better identify metonymy in hybrid sentences than in metonymy-only sentences, demonstrating that the presence of a metaphor makes a metonymic noun more explicit. Our dataset is publicly available at: https://github.com/cincynlp/MetFuse.

</details>


### [51] [MoshiRAG: Asynchronous Knowledge Retrieval for Full-Duplex Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12928)
*Chung-Ming Chien,Manu Orsini,Eugene Kharitonov,Neil Zeghidour,Karen Livescu,Alexandre Défossez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MoshiRAG：一种结合紧凑型全双工语音接口与选择性检索的模块化方法，在保持实时交互性的同时提升语音对话模型的事实准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音语言模型虽然具有实时交互性（处理停顿、打断、反馈等），但事实准确性不足。单纯扩大模型规模虽能改善准确性，但会导致实时推理成本过高，因此需要一种既能保持交互性又能提升事实准确性的解决方案。

Method: 提出MoshiRAG模块化框架：1）使用紧凑型全双工接口；2）结合选择性检索机制访问更强大的知识源；3）采用异步框架，让模型识别需要知识的查询并将回答基于外部信息；4）利用响应开始与核心信息传递之间的自然时间间隔完成检索，保持对话流畅性。

Result: MoshiRAG在事实准确性上达到最佳公开非双工语音语言模型水平，同时保持了全双工系统的交互特性。其灵活设计支持即插即用的检索方法而无需重新训练，并在领域外数学推理任务上表现出色。

Conclusion: MoshiRAG通过模块化设计成功解决了全双工语音语言模型的事实准确性难题，在保持实时交互性的同时显著提升了模型的知识可靠性，为构建更自然、更准确的语音对话系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech language models have recently emerged to enhance the naturalness of conversational AI. In particular, full-duplex models are distinguished by their real-time interactivity, including handling of pauses, interruptions, and backchannels. However, improving their factuality remains an open challenge. While scaling the model size could address this gap, it would make real-time inference prohibitively expensive. In this work, we propose MoshiRAG, a modular approach that combines a compact full-duplex interface with selective retrieval to access more powerful knowledge sources. Our asynchronous framework enables the model to identify knowledge-demanding queries and ground its responses in external information. By leveraging the natural temporal gap between response onset and the delivery of core information, the retrieval process can be completed while maintaining a natural conversation flow. With this approach, MoshiRAG achieves factuality comparable to the best publicly released non-duplex speech language models while preserving the interactivity inherent to full-duplex systems. Moreover, our flexible design supports plug-and-play retrieval methods without retraining and demonstrates strong performance on out-of-domain mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [52] [GlotOCR Bench: OCR Models Still Struggle Beyond a Handful of Unicode Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12978)
*Amir Hossein Kargaran,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Jana Diesner,François Yvon,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GlotOCR Bench是一个包含100多种Unicode文字的综合OCR基准测试，评估模型在干净和退化图像上的泛化能力，发现现有模型仅能在少数文字上表现良好，最强模型也无法泛化到30种以上文字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OCR评估主要集中在少数高资源和中资源文字上，缺乏对多种Unicode文字泛化能力的全面评估，需要建立一个更全面的基准测试来评估OCR模型在多文字场景下的真实性能。

Method: 从真实多语言文本生成干净和退化图像变体，使用Google Fonts字体库，通过HarfBuzz进行字形排布，FreeType进行光栅化，支持LTR和RTL文字。手动检查所有文字的渲染正确性，评估开源和专有视觉语言模型。

Result: 大多数模型在少于10种文字上表现良好，即使最强的前沿模型也无法泛化到30种以上文字。性能与文字级别的预训练覆盖度相关，模型面对不熟悉的文字时会产生随机噪声或从已知相似文字中产生幻觉字符。

Conclusion: 当前OCR系统严重依赖语言模型预训练而非视觉识别能力，需要更全面的多文字评估基准。发布了基准测试和可复现的代码管道。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) has advanced rapidly with the rise of vision-language models, yet evaluation has remained concentrated on a small cluster of high- and mid-resource scripts. We introduce GlotOCR Bench, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating OCR generalization across 100+ Unicode scripts. Our benchmark comprises clean and degraded image variants rendered from real multilingual texts. Images are rendered using fonts from the Google Fonts repository, shaped with HarfBuzz and rasterized with FreeType, supporting both LTR and RTL scripts. Samples of rendered images were manually reviewed to verify correct rendering across all scripts. We evaluate a broad suite of open-weight and proprietary vision-language models and find that most perform well on fewer than ten scripts, and even the strongest frontier models fail to generalize beyond thirty scripts. Performance broadly tracks script-level pretraining coverage, suggesting that current OCR systems rely on language model pretraining as much as on visual recognition. Models confronted with unfamiliar scripts either produce random noise or hallucinate characters from similar scripts they already know. We release the benchmark and pipeline for reproducibility. Pipeline Code: https://github.com/cisnlp/glotocr-bench, Benchmark: https://hf.co/datasets/cis-lmu/glotocr-bench.

</details>


### [53] [PolicyLLM: Towards Excellent Comprehension of Public Policy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12995)
*Han Bao,Penghao Zhang,Yue Huang,Zhengqing Yuan,Yanchi Ru,Rui Su,Yujun Zhou,Xiangqi Wang,Kehan Guo,Nitesh V Chawla,Yanfang Ye,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolicyBench：首个大规模跨系统（中美）政策理解基准，包含21K案例，评估记忆、理解和应用三个认知层次；PolicyMoE：针对政策任务的领域专用MoE模型，在应用任务上表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地应用于现实世界决策（包括公共政策领域），但其理解和推理政策相关内容的能力尚未得到充分探索。需要填补这一空白，评估LLMs在政策领域的认知能力。

Method: 1. 构建PolicyBench基准：大规模跨系统（美国-中国）政策理解基准，包含21,000个案例，覆盖广泛政策领域，捕捉现实治理的多样性和复杂性。2. 基于布鲁姆分类学评估三个核心能力：记忆（事实回忆）、理解（概念和情境推理）、应用（现实政策场景中的问题解决）。3. 提出PolicyMoE：领域专用的混合专家模型，专家模块与每个认知层次对齐。

Result: 1. 提出的模型在面向应用的政策任务上表现优于记忆或概念理解任务。2. 在结构化推理任务上获得最高准确率。3. 结果揭示了当前LLMs在政策理解方面的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前LLMs在政策理解方面的不足，为开发更可靠、专注于政策领域的模型指明了路径。PolicyBench为评估政策相关AI能力提供了重要基准，PolicyMoE展示了领域专用架构在提升应用任务性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into real-world decision-making, including in the domain of public policy. Yet, their ability to comprehend and reason about policy-related content remains underexplored. To fill this gap, we present \textbf{\textit{PolicyBench}}, the first large-scale cross-system benchmark (US-China) evaluating policy comprehension, comprising 21K cases across a broad spectrum of policy areas, capturing the diversity and complexity of real-world governance. Following Bloom's taxonomy, the benchmark assesses three core capabilities: (1) \textbf{Memorization}: factual recall of policy knowledge, (2) \textbf{Understanding}: conceptual and contextual reasoning, and (3) \textbf{Application}: problem-solving in real-life policy scenarios. Building on this benchmark, we further propose \textbf{\textit{PolicyMoE}}, a domain-specialized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with expert modules aligned to each cognitive level. The proposed models demonstrate stronger performance on application-oriented policy tasks than on memorization or conceptual understanding, and yields the highest accuracy on structured reasoning tasks. Our results reveal key limitations of current LLMs in policy understanding and suggest paths toward more reliable, policy-focused models.

</details>


### [54] [One Token Away from Collapse: The Fragility of Instruction-Tuned Helpfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13006)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 指令调优的大语言模型在简单词汇约束下（如禁止单个标点或常用词）会严重退化，导致回答完整性下降14-48%，揭示了指令调优带来的脆弱性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究指令调优大语言模型的鲁棒性，探究当受到简单词汇约束时，这些模型是否仍能保持其帮助性和完整性。先前研究主要关注格式级约束，而本研究关注更细微的词汇级约束对模型表现的影响。

Method: 对三个开源模型家族和一个闭源模型（GPT-4o-mini）进行实验，施加简单词汇约束（禁止单个标点或常用词）。采用成对评估方法，由GPT-4o-mini和GPT-4o进行1920次比较。进行机制分析，包括两阶段生成（自由生成后约束重写）和线性探针分析提示表示。在MT-Bench的八个任务类别上进行验证。

Result: 简单词汇约束导致指令调优模型回答完整性下降14-48%，基线回答在77-100%的成对比较中更受偏好。GPT-4o-mini完整性损失31%（基线胜率99%）。两阶段生成可恢复59-96%的回答长度。线性探针在提示表示中可预测回答长度（R²=0.51-0.93），而基础模型R²为负值。基础模型在相同约束下无系统性退化。标准独立LLM评估仅检测到3.5%质量下降，而成对评估揭示23%下降。

Conclusion: 指令调优使大语言模型对简单词汇约束异常脆弱，这种脆弱性源于指令调优将任务能力与狭窄的表面形式模板耦合。机制分析表明这是规划失败问题。标准评估方法存在盲点，无法充分检测约束生成的质量下降。研究揭示了指令调优模型的系统性脆弱性，对模型鲁棒性评估和部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned large language models produce helpful, structured responses, but how robust is this helpfulness when trivially constrained? We show that simple lexical constraints (banning a single punctuation character or common word) cause instruction-tuned LLMs to collapse their responses, losing 14--48% of comprehensiveness in pairwise evaluation across three open-weight model families and one closed-weight model (GPT-4o-mini). The baseline response is preferred in 77--100% of 1,920 pairwise comparisons judged by GPT-4o-mini and GPT-4o. Notably, GPT-4o-mini suffers 31% comprehensiveness loss (99% baseline win rate), demonstrating that the fragility extends to commercially deployed closed-weight models, contrary to prior findings on format-level constraints. Through mechanistic analysis, we identify this as a planning failure: two-pass generation (free generation followed by constrained rewriting) recovers 59--96% of response length, and linear probes on prompt representations predict response length with $R^2 = 0.51$--$0.93$ before generation begins, with $R^2$ tracking collapse severity across models. The same probes yield negative $R^2$ on base models, confirming that instruction tuning creates the representational structure encoding the collapse decision. Crucially, base models show no systematic collapse under identical constraints, with effects that are small, noisy, and bidirectional, demonstrating that instruction tuning creates this fragility by coupling task competence to narrow surface-form templates. The effect replicates on MT-Bench across all eight task categories. We further show that standard independent LLM-as-judge evaluation detects only a 3.5% average quality drop where pairwise evaluation reveals 23%, exposing a methodological blind spot in how constrained generation is assessed.

</details>


### [55] [Toward Autonomous Long-Horizon Engineering for ML Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13018)
*Guoxin Chen,Jie Chen,Lei Chen,Jiale Zhao,Fanzhe Meng,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Cheng Chen,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AiScientist系统通过分层编排和持久状态连续性实现自主长周期ML研究工程，在PaperBench和MLE-Bench Lite基准上显著优于基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI研究快速发展，但长周期ML研究工程仍然困难：智能体需要在任务理解、环境设置、实现、实验和调试等方面维持数小时或数天的连贯进展

Method: AiScientist基于结构化编排和持久状态连续性原则，结合分层编排和基于权限的File-as-Bus工作空间：顶层编排器通过简洁摘要和工作空间映射维持阶段级控制，而专门化智能体在持久工件（分析、计划、代码、实验证据）上重复接地，而非主要依赖对话交接

Result: 在两个互补基准上，AiScientist在PaperBench上平均比最佳匹配基线提高10.54分，在MLE-Bench Lite上达到81.82% Any Medal。消融研究表明File-as-Bus协议是关键性能驱动因素，移除后PaperBench下降6.41分，MLE-Bench Lite下降31.82分

Conclusion: 长周期ML研究工程是协调专门化工作在持久项目状态上的系统问题，而非纯粹的局部推理问题

Abstract: Autonomous AI research has advanced rapidly, but long-horizon ML research engineering remains difficult: agents must sustain coherent progress across task comprehension, environment setup, implementation, experimentation, and debugging over hours or days. We introduce AiScientist, a system for autonomous long-horizon engineering for ML research built on a simple principle: strong long-horizon performance requires both structured orchestration and durable state continuity. To this end, AiScientist combines hierarchical orchestration with a permission-scoped File-as-Bus workspace: a top-level Orchestrator maintains stage-level control through concise summaries and a workspace map, while specialized agents repeatedly re-ground on durable artifacts such as analyses, plans, code, and experimental evidence rather than relying primarily on conversational handoffs, yielding thin control over thick state. Across two complementary benchmarks, AiScientist improves PaperBench score by 10.54 points on average over the best matched baseline and achieves 81.82 Any Medal% on MLE-Bench Lite. Ablation studies further show that File-as-Bus protocol is a key driver of performance, reducing PaperBench by 6.41 points and MLE-Bench Lite by 31.82 points when removed. These results suggest that long-horizon ML research engineering is a systems problem of coordinating specialized work over durable project state, rather than a purely local reasoning problem.

</details>


### [56] [Back to Basics: Let Conversational Agents Remember with Just Retrieval and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11628)
*Yuqian Wu,Wei Chen,Zhengjun Huang,Junle Chen,Qingxiang Liu,Kai Wang,Xiaofang Zhou,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MEM方法，通过Turn Isolation Retrieval和Query-Driven Pruning解决对话记忆中的信号稀疏问题，实现高效紧凑的对话记忆管理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话记忆系统依赖复杂的层次化摘要或强化学习，但随着对话增长容易出现上下文稀释问题。研究发现瓶颈可能在于潜在知识流形中的"信号稀疏效应"，包括决定性证据稀疏和双重冗余问题

Method: 提出MEM框架，包含Turn Isolation Retrieval（TIR）和Query-Driven Pruning（QDP）。TIR采用最大激活策略捕获轮次级信号，替代全局聚合；QDP移除冗余会话和对话填充内容，构建紧凑的高密度证据集

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，MEM在不同设置下均实现稳健性能，持续优于强基线方法，同时在token数量和延迟方面保持高效率

Conclusion: MEM为对话记忆建立了新的极简主义基线，通过直接检索和生成方法有效解决了信号稀疏问题，证明了简单但精心设计的框架可以超越复杂架构

Abstract: Existing conversational memory systems rely on complex hierarchical summarization or reinforcement learning to manage long-term dialogue history, yet remain vulnerable to context dilution as conversations grow. In this work, we offer a different perspective: the primary bottleneck may lie not in memory architecture, but in the \textit{Signal Sparsity Effect} within the latent knowledge manifold. Through controlled experiments, we identify two key phenomena: \textit{Decisive Evidence Sparsity}, where relevant signals become increasingly isolated with longer sessions, leading to sharp degradation in aggregation-based methods; and \textit{Dual-Level Redundancy}, where both inter-session interference and intra-session conversational filler introduce large amounts of non-informative content, hindering effective generation. Motivated by these insights, we propose \method, a minimalist framework that brings conversational memory back to basics, relying solely on retrieval and generation via Turn Isolation Retrieval (TIR) and Query-Driven Pruning (QDP). TIR replaces global aggregation with a max-activation strategy to capture turn-level signals, while QDP removes redundant sessions and conversational filler to construct a compact, high-density evidence set. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \method achieves robust performance across diverse settings, consistently outperforming strong baselines while maintaining high efficiency in tokens and latency, establishing a new minimalist baseline for conversational memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

### [57] [Evaluating Lightweight Block Cipher Payload Encryption for Real-Time CAN Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11853)
*Kevin Setterstrom,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在QT PY ESP32-S2微控制器上实现轻量级分组密码对CAN总线负载进行加密，评估其防止基于语义分类的逆向工程的能力，同时保持100Hz传输频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAN总线通信通常未加密，攻击者可通过观察未加密流量进行统计分析和语义推断（语义分类逆向工程）。需要评估在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上实现加密的可行性，以保护信号语义不被被动推断。

Method: 使用QT PY ESP32-S2微控制器实现轻量级分组密码对CAN负载进行加密。通过实验评估时序影响、负载模式可观测性和基于相关性的推断能力。测量加密对传输调度的影响。

Result: 加密成功掩盖了常数值和可预测的信号模式，同时保持了100Hz的传输调度。轻量级加密在资源受限硬件上仅产生有限的时序开销，有效减少了基于被动观察的CAN信号语义推断。

Conclusion: 轻量级负载加密可在资源受限的嵌入式CAN节点上实现，有效防止基于语义分类的逆向工程，同时保持实时传输要求，为CAN总线安全提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This study evaluates the feasibility of integrating lightweight block cipher payload encryption into a real-time embedded controller area network (CAN) node using a QT PY ESP32-S2 microcontroller. This work seeks to determine whether the use of a block cipher can prevent semantic taxonomy-based reverse engineering, which infers signal meaning from unencrypted CAN traffic using observation and statistical analysis. CAN payloads are encrypted using a lightweight block cipher and evaluated through experiments that measure timing impact, payload pattern observability, and correlation-based inference. Results indicate that encryption masks constant values and predictable signal patterns while preserving a 100 Hz transmission schedule. These findings suggest that lightweight payload encryption can reduce passive, observation based inference of CAN signal semantics on resource-constrained hardware with limited timing overhead impact.

</details>


### [58] [Can we Watermark Low-Entropy LLM Outputs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12051)
*Noam Mazor,Andrew Morgan,Rafael Pass*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型输出水印技术，特别关注在每令牌熵仅为常数（而非依赖字母表大小）的低熵条件下，构建可证明不可检测的水印方案，并实现对抗随机替换和删除的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可证明不可检测的水印方案（如Christ等人和Golowich等人的工作）要求输出具有恒定的熵率，即足够长子串中恒定比例的令牌需要具有O(log|T|)的经验熵。本文旨在探索在每令牌熵仅为常数（不依赖字母表大小或安全参数）的低熵条件下，是否也能实现鲁棒的水印方案。

Method: 在低熵条件下构建两种水印方案：1）基于亚指数LPN假设（与Christ等人相同），构建对抗随机替换的鲁棒水印方案；2）构建对抗随机替换和随机删除的鲁棒水印方案，需要额外启发式假设（LLM输出仅引入随机错误）或构建伪随机纠错码。

Result: 成功在每令牌熵仅为常数的低熵条件下构建了水印方案：第一种方案能抵抗随机替换攻击（基于亚指数LPN假设）；第二种方案能抵抗随机替换和随机删除攻击（需额外假设或伪随机纠错码）。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使在每令牌熵仅为常数的低熵条件下，也能构建可证明不可检测且具有一定鲁棒性的水印方案，扩展了现有水印技术的适用范围，突破了先前工作对恒定熵率的强假设要求。

Abstract: A recent and exciting thread of work focuses on developing methods for watermarking the output of large language models (LLMs). We focus on provably undetectable watermarking-that is, schemes that do not alter the output distribution of the LLM, yet enable embedding a watermark in the output that identifies the output as having been generated by the particular LLM. Furthermore, the watermark should be hard to remove by an adversary that may potentially edit, insert, or delete tokens from the watermarked output. Indeed, recent work (Christ et al. [COLT'24], Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24], Golowich et al. [NeuroIPS'24]) shows how to develop such schemes that are robust against a constant fraction of substitutions, or even against a constant fraction of arbitrary edits.
  These works, however, make strong assumptions on the entropy present in the output of the LLM. Most notably, they all require constant entropy rate-that is, a constant fraction of the tokens in a sufficiently long substring of the output need to have empirical entropy at least O(log |T|), where T is the alphabet of tokens, and Golowich et al. additionally require T to be larger than the security parameter. In this work, we consider whether we can also watermark the outputs of LLMs when the per-token entropy is just a constant, discarding the dependence on the alphabet size or security parameter. In this regime, we construct:
  - A watermarking scheme robust against random substitutions (assuming subexponential LPN, as in Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24])
  - A watermarking scheme robust against random substitutions and random deletions, given either the additional heuristic assumption that the output of the LLM only introduces random errors (analogous to the assumption made by Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24]) or a construction of a pseudorandom error-correcting code robust to adversarial substitutions and random deletions.

</details>


### [59] [Fully Homomorphic Encryption on Llama 3 model for privacy preserving LLM inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12168)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将后量子密码学（PQC）中的格基同态加密（HE）集成到LLM推理管道中，以保护LLaMA-3模型的数据隐私，防止量子计算攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI与数据驱动领域的结合带来了效率提升，但也引发了LLM安全性和数据隐私的严重担忧。现有安全技术无法抵御量子计算攻击，量子计算可能破解现有加密算法，导致密钥泄露和敏感数据解密。

Method: 修改LLaMA-3模型的Transformer架构推理管道，注入concrete-ml库提供的同态加密主要操作，将后量子密码学（PQC）中的格基同态加密（HE）集成到LLM推理层中。

Result: 在i9 CPU上实现了高达98%的文本生成准确率，延迟为237毫秒，达到每秒80个令牌的生成速度，证明了FHE保护的LLaMA-3推理模型的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将后量子同态加密集成到LLM推理管道中，可以有效保护模型免受数据隐私攻击，特别是抵御未来量子计算威胁，同时保持合理的性能表现。

Abstract: The applications of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) and their intersections with data-driven fields, such as healthcare, finance, transportation, and information security, have led to significant improvements in service efficiency and low latency. However, this synergy raises serious concerns regarding the security of large language models (LLMs) and their potential impact on the privacy of companies and users' data. Many technology companies that incorporate LLMs in their services with a certain level of command and control bear a risk of data exposure and secret divulgence caused by insecure LLM pipelines, making them vulnerable to multiple attacks such as data poisoning, prompt injection, and model theft. Although several security techniques (input/output sanitization, decentralized learning, access control management, and encryption) were implemented to reduce this risk, there is still an imminent risk of quantum computing attacks, which are expected to break existing encryption algorithms, hence, retrieving secret keys, encrypted sensitive data, and decrypting encrypted models. In this extensive work, we integrate the Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) based Lattice-based Homomorphic Encryption (HE) main functions in the LLM's inference pipeline to secure some of its layers against data privacy attacks. We modify the inference pipeline of the transformer architecture for the LLAMA-3 model while injecting the main homomorphic encryption operations provided by the concrete-ml library. We demonstrate high text generation accuracies (up to 98%) with reasonable latencies (237 ms) on an i9 CPU, reaching up to 80 tokens per second, which proves the feasibility and validity of our work while running a FHE-secured LLAMA-3 inference model. Further experiments and analysis are discussed to justify models' text generation latencies and behaviours.

</details>


### [60] [COBALT-TLA: A Neuro-Symbolic Verification Loop for Cross-Chain Bridge Vulnerability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12172)
*Dominik Blain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COBALT-TLA是一个神经符号验证循环，将LLM与TLA+模型检查器TLC配对，通过自动REPL实现有界TLA+规范的生成、验证和错误修复的收敛过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM在形式化方法中的幻觉问题，通过确定性的证明器反馈将零样本代码生成转化为收敛的证明发现策略，提高跨链桥等关键系统的形式化验证效率。

Method: 构建神经符号验证循环：LLM生成有界TLA+规范，TLC作为语义预言机进行模型检查，结构化错误轨迹被解析并注入模型上下文以驱动规范收敛。

Result: 在三个跨链桥目标（包括190M美元的Nomad漏洞模型）上评估，COBALT-TLA最多2次迭代即可达到BUG_FOUND状态，TLC执行时间始终低于0.30秒，并自主发现了未在人工规范中出现的Optimistic Relay Attack漏洞类型。

Conclusion: 确定性的证明器反馈足以消除LLM在形式化方法中的幻觉，将零样本代码生成转化为收敛的证明发现策略，神经符号验证循环在自动漏洞发现方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: We present COBALT-TLA, a neuro-symbolic verification loop that pairs an LLM with TLC, the TLA+ model checker, in an automated REPL. The LLM generates bounded TLA+ specifications; TLC acts as a semantic oracle; structured error traces are parsed and injected back into the model's context to drive convergence. We evaluate the system against three cross-chain bridge targets, including a faithful model of the Nomad $190M exploit. COBALT-TLA reaches a verified BUG_FOUND state in at most 2 iterations on all targets, with TLC execution consistently below 0.30 seconds. Notably, the system autonomously discovers an unprompted vulnerability class -- the Optimistic Relay Attack -- not present in the human-written baseline specification. We argue that deterministic prover feedback is sufficient to neutralize LLM hallucination in formal methods, transforming zero-shot code generation into a convergent proof-finding strategy.

</details>


### [61] [Mitigating S-RAHA: An On-device Framework to Prevent Forwarding of Re-Captured Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12178)
*Keshav Sood,Iynkaran Natgunanathan,Purathani Praitheeshan,Praitheeshan Kirupananthan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Screen Recaptured Analog Hole Attack (S-RAHA)攻击，即通过另一台手机拍摄屏幕来绕过数字截图防护，并开发了基于深度学习的设备端重捕获检测与防护框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动应用隐私保护机制主要针对数字截图检测，但无法防止物理层面的屏幕重捕获攻击。攻击者使用另一台智能手机拍摄屏幕，可以绕过所有数字防护措施，导致敏感图像被非法转发，带来严重的隐私、人身安全和取证风险。

Method: 提出低计算量的设备端安全框架：1) 深度学习辅助的重捕获检测模型，能在不同环境条件下区分原始数字内容和屏幕重捕获图像；2) 设备端执行机制，自动阻止可疑重捕获图像在应用间共享；3) 引入不可见元数据标识符(IMI)概念，嵌入受保护图像中实现取证溯源。

Result: 开发了能够检测物理屏幕重捕获攻击的系统框架，虽然IMI组件主要在概念和可行性层面探索而非完全实现，但展示了在客户端安全架构中集成轻量级不可见标识符的可行方向。

Conclusion: S-RAHA攻击暴露了现有数字防护方案在物理层面的安全漏洞，提出的设备端框架为解决这一问题提供了可行方案，通过深度学习检测、自动阻止共享和取证溯源相结合，增强了移动应用对物理屏幕重捕获攻击的防护能力。

Abstract: Protecting sensitive visual content from unauthorized redistribution is a growing challenge for privacy focused mobile applications, including dating platforms. Screenshot prevention mechanisms, rely on server side monitoring or are limited to digital screenshot detection, are commonly deployed to stop forwarding sensitive images. However, an adversary uses another smartphone to take a photo of the mobile screen, in this scenario the existing solutions offer no protection against psychically screen recapture attacks. Since the attack happens in the physical plane rather than on a digital plane and shows a void or hole in the existing solutions, we name this the Screen Recaptured Analog Hole Attack (S RAHA). Such physically recaptured images bypass digital safeguards and can be freely forwarded, creating substantial privacy, personal safety, and forensic risks. We present a low computational secure by design on device framework that aims to detect and prevent the forwarding of recaptured images directly to the users device. The proposed system integrates a deep learning assisted recapture detection model capable of distinguishing original digital content from camera to screen captures under diverse environmental conditions, together with an on device enforcement mechanism that automatically blocks the sharing of suspected recaptured images between applications. We also introduce the concept of an invisible metadata identifier (IMI) that can be embedded into protected images to enable forensic traceability of potential leakage paths. Although the IMI component is explored at a conceptual and feasibility level rather than fully implemented, it demonstrates a promising direction for integrating lightweight, invisible identifiers into client side security architectures.

</details>


### [62] [TimeMark: A Trustworthy Time Watermarking Framework for Exact Generation-Time Recovery from AIGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12216)
*Shangkun Che,Silin Du,Ge Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出可信水印框架，解决LLM生成内容知识产权纠纷，实现100%时间戳恢复精度，抵抗用户统计攻击和提供商伪造


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在文本生成中的广泛应用引发知识产权纠纷担忧。现有水印技术依赖token分布的统计信号，导致概率性检测和可靠性降低，特别是在多比特编码（如时间戳）时。这些方法引入可检测的统计模式，易受伪造攻击，且允许模型提供商伪造任意水印。

Method: 提出可信水印概念，专注于可信时间水印作为司法证据。框架整合密码学技术，在监管监督下将时间信息编码为时间依赖的密钥，防止任意时间戳伪造。水印载荷与时间解耦，为每个实例生成随机、非存储的比特序列，消除统计模式。设计两阶段编码机制，结合纠错码，实现理论上完美的生成时间恢复精度。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明，该框架满足司法证据的可靠性要求，为未来AIGC相关知识产权纠纷提供实用解决方案。实现100%识别准确率，可靠恢复生成时间，同时抵抗用户端统计攻击和提供商端伪造。

Conclusion: 可信水印框架通过密码学技术和两阶段编码机制，解决了现有水印技术的可靠性问题，为LLM生成内容的知识产权保护提供了可靠的司法证据解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The widespread use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in text generation has raised increasing concerns about intellectual property disputes. Watermarking techniques, which embed meta information into AI-generated content (AIGC), have the potential to serve as judicial evidence. However, existing methods rely on statistical signals in token distributions, leading to inherently probabilistic detection and reduced reliability, especially in multi-bit encoding (e.g., timestamps). Moreover, such methods introduce detectable statistical patterns, making them vulnerable to forgery attacks and enabling model providers to fabricate arbitrary watermarks. To address these issues, we propose the concept of trustworthy watermark, which achieves reliable recovery with 100% identification accuracy while resisting both user-side statistical attacks and provider-side forgery. We focus on trustworthy time watermarking for use as judicial evidence. Our framework integrates cryptographic techniques and encodes time information into time-dependent secret keys under regulatory supervision, preventing arbitrary timestamp fabrication. The watermark payload is decoupled from time and generated as a random, non-stored bit sequence for each instance, eliminating statistical patterns. To ensure verifiability, we design a two-stage encoding mechanism, which, combined with error-correcting codes, enables reliable recovery of generation time with theoretically perfect accuracy. Both theoretical analysis and experiments demonstrate that our framework satisfies the reliability requirements for judicial evidence and offers a practical solution for future AIGC-related intellectual property disputes.

</details>


### [63] [From IOCs to Regex: Automating CTI Operationalization for SOC with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12228)
*Pei-Yu Tseng,Lan Zhang,ZihDwo Yeh,Xiaoyan Sun,Xushu Dai,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IOCRegex-gen：基于LLM的自动化正则表达式生成系统，可将CTI报告中的IOC转换为正则表达式，解决手动构建正则表达式的效率低下问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CTI报告中IOC的正则表达式构建主要依赖人工，过程缓慢、易错且难以应对日益增长的CTI数据量。现有LLM方法通常只输出普通字符串而非正则表达式，无法有效捕获系统上下文、日志格式或攻击者行为的变体。

Method: 提出IOCRegex-gen系统，包含两个关键创新：(1) 组感知机制，识别IOC片段应作为捕获组还是非捕获组；(2) 迭代推理和多阶段验证管道，确保语法有效性和语义正确性。

Result: 在超过3,000份真实CTI报告和2,400个来自MITRE ATT&CK评估框架的基准字符串上测试，IOCRegex-gen平均命中率达到99.1%，误报率仅为0.8%。

Conclusion: IOCRegex-gen能够有效支持大规模CTI处理和自动化正则表达式生成，显著提高安全运营效率。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports contain Indicators of Compromise (IOCs) that are critical for security operations. To operationalize these IOCs across heterogeneous logs, analysts often convert them into regular expressions (regexes) for tasks such as digital forensics, log parsing, and SIEM rule creation. However, regex construction is still largely manual, requiring analysts to extract IOCs from CTI reports and transform them into syntactically valid and semantically precise patterns. This process is slow, error-prone, and increasingly impractical as CTI volumes grow.
  Although recent studies have applied Large Language Models (LLMs) to IOC extraction, they typically output plain strings rather than regexes, limiting practical deployment. Plain IOCs cannot effectively capture variations in system context, log format, or attacker behavior.
  To address this gap, we propose IOCRegex-gen, a fully automated LLM-based regex generation system that converts IOCs into regexes. The system introduces two key innovations: (i) a group-aware mechanism that identifies which IOC segments should be represented as capture or non-capture groups, and (ii) an iterative reasoning and multi-stage validation pipeline to ensure syntactic validity and semantic correctness.
  Experiments on over 3,000 real CTI reports and 2,400 ground-truth strings from the MITRE ATT&CK Evaluation framework show that IOCRegex-gen achieves an average hit rate of 99.1% and a false-positive rate of only 0.8%, demonstrating its effectiveness for large-scale CTI processing and automated regex generation.

</details>


### [64] [TEMPLATEFUZZ: Fine-Grained Chat Template Fuzzing for Jailbreaking and Red Teaming LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12232)
*Qingchao Shen,Zibo Xiao,Lili Huang,Enwei Hu,Yongqiang Tian,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TEMPLATEFUZZ是一个针对LLM聊天模板的细粒度模糊测试框架，通过元素级变异规则、启发式搜索和主动学习策略，有效发现聊天模板中的安全漏洞，在开源和商业LLM上均取得高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究主要关注提示注入攻击，需要大量提示工程且忽略了聊天模板这一关键攻击面。聊天模板作为LLM部署中的重要组件，其安全漏洞尚未得到充分探索，存在显著安全风险。

Method: 提出TEMPLATEFUZZ框架：1) 设计元素级变异规则生成多样化的聊天模板变体；2) 采用启发式搜索策略引导模板生成方向，在保持模型准确性的同时提高攻击成功率；3) 集成基于主动学习的轻量级规则oracle，用于高效评估越狱攻击。

Result: 在12个开源LLM上，平均攻击成功率达98.2%，准确率仅下降1.1%，优于现有方法9.1%-47.9%的攻击成功率和8.4%的准确率下降。在5个商业LLM上，通过聊天模板提示注入攻击实现90%的平均攻击成功率。

Conclusion: TEMPLATEFUZZ证明了聊天模板是LLM安全的重要攻击面，其细粒度模糊测试方法能有效发现模板级漏洞，为LLM安全评估提供了新视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across diverse domains, yet their vulnerability to jailbreak attacks, where adversarial inputs bypass safety mechanisms to elicit harmful outputs, poses significant security risks. While prior work has primarily focused on prompt injection attacks, these approaches often require resource-intensive prompt engineering and overlook other critical components, such as chat templates. This paper introduces TEMPLATEFUZZ, a fine-grained fuzzing framework that systematically exposes vulnerabilities in chat templates, a critical yet underexplored attack surface in LLMs. Specifically, TEMPLATEFUZZ (1) designs a series of element-level mutation rules to generate diverse chat template variants, (2) proposes a heuristic search strategy to guide the chat template generation toward the direction of amplifying the attack success rate (ASR) while preserving model accuracy, and (3) integrates an active learning-based strategy to derive a lightweight rule-based oracle for accurate and efficient jailbreak evaluation. Evaluated on twelve open-source LLMs across multiple attack scenarios, TEMPLATEFUZZ achieves an average ASR of 98.2% with only 1.1% accuracy degradation, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%-47.9% in ASR and 8.4% in accuracy degradation. Moreover, even on five industry-leading commercial LLMs where chat templates cannot be specified, TEMPLATEFUZZ attains a 90% average ASR via chat template-based prompt injection attacks.

</details>


### [65] [SpanKey: Dynamic Key Space Conditioning for Neural Network Access Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12254)
*WenBin Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpanKey是一种轻量级门控推理方法，通过将密钥注入激活值来控制模型访问，无需加密权重或追求门控推理的排行榜精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统门控推理方法通常需要加密权重或追求高精度，计算开销大。SpanKey旨在提供一种轻量级替代方案，通过密钥控制模型访问，降低计算成本。

Method: 定义低维密钥子空间Span(B)，训练时采样系数α生成密钥k=α⊤B，通过加性或乘性映射将密钥注入中间激活值。使用有效密钥（在子空间内）和无效密钥（在子空间外）进行训练，引入拒绝损失函数防止密钥吸收。

Result: 在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18和MNIST数据集上进行了实验，展示了三种工作模式（A-C）及其扩展。分析了密钥吸收问题，提出了Beta能量分割和边缘尾部诊断方法，但未提供严格的安全性证明。

Conclusion: SpanKey为门控推理提供了一种轻量级框架，通过密钥子空间注入实现访问控制。虽然存在密钥吸收等挑战，但通过拒绝损失函数可以缓解。该方法不承诺密码学安全性，但为可扩展的门控推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: SpanKey is a lightweight way to gate inference without encrypting weights or chasing leaderboard accuracy on gated inference. The idea is to condition activations on secret keys. A basis matrix $B$ defines a low-dimensional key subspace $Span(B)$; during training we sample coefficients $α$ and form keys $k=α^\top B$, then inject them into intermediate activations with additive or multiplicative maps and strength $γ$. Valid keys lie in $Span(B)$; invalid keys are sampled outside that subspace. We make three points. (i) Mechanism: subspace key injection and a multi-layer design space. (ii) Failure mode: key absorption, together with two analytical results (a Beta-energy split and margin-tail diagnostics), explains weak baseline separation in energy and margin terms -- these are not a security theorem. iii) Deny losses and experiments: Modes A--C and extensions, with CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 runs and MNIST ablations for Mode B. We summarize setup and first-order analysis, injectors, absorption, deny losses and ablations, a threat discussion that does not promise cryptography, and closing remarks on scale. Code: \texttt{https://github.com/mindmemory-ai/dksc}

</details>


### [66] [UniDetect: LLM-Driven Universal Fraud Detection across Heterogeneous Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12329)
*Shuyi Miao,Wangjie Qiu,Shengda Zhuo,Fei Shen,Dan Lin,Xingtong Yu,Chua Tat-Seng,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UniDetect是一种基于大语言模型的多链加密货币欺诈账户检测方法，通过领域知识指导LLM生成通用交易摘要文本作为欺诈检测证据，采用两阶段交替训练策略增强多模态联合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着跨链互操作性发展，DeFi协议使非法资金能够重组为统一的流动性资产并在加密货币市场流动，这些操作可以绕过针对单个区块链的监控，从而削弱当前监管框架。

Method: 1. 使用领域知识指导LLM生成适用于异构区块链账户的通用交易摘要文本，作为欺诈账户检测的证据；2. 引入两阶段交替训练策略，持续动态增强基于文本证据和交易图模式的多模态联合推理能力。

Result: 1. 在多个区块链上的实验显示，UniDetect在Kolmogorov-Smirnov(KS)指标上比现有方法提升5.57%到7.58%；2. 在跨链零样本检测中，UniDetect识别超过94.58%的欺诈账户；3. 在非区块链数据上也表现良好，F1分数比现有方法提高6.06%。

Conclusion: UniDetect通过LLM生成交易摘要文本和两阶段训练策略，有效解决了跨链欺诈账户检测问题，在多个区块链和跨链场景中表现出色，并能泛化到非区块链数据，为加密货币监管提供了有效工具。

Abstract: As cross-chain interoperability advances, decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols enable illicit funds to be reorganized into uniform liquid assets that flow throughout the cryptocurrency market. Such operations can bypass monitoring targeted at individual blockchains and thereby weaken current regulatory frameworks. Motivated by these, we introduce UniDetect, a multi-chain cryptocurrency fraud account detection method based on large language models (LLMs). Specifically, we use domain knowledge to guide the LLM to generate general transaction summary texts applicable to heterogeneous blockchain accounts, which serve as evidence for fraud account detection. Furthermore, we introduce a two-stage alternating training strategy to continuously and dynamically enhance the multimodal joint reasoning for detecting fraudulent accounts based on both the textual evidence and the transaction graph patterns. Experiments on multiple blockchains show that UniDetect outperforms existing methods 5.57% to 7.58% in Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS). For cross-chain zero-shot detection, UniDetect identifies over 94.58% of fraudulent accounts. It also generalizes well to non-blockchain data, delivering a 6.06% improvement in F1 over existing methods. The dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/msy0513/UniDetect.

</details>


### [67] [CoLA: A Choice Leakage Attack Framework to Expose Privacy Risks in Subset Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12342)
*Qi Li,Cheng-Long Wang,Yinzhi Cao,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了"训练数据子集能降低隐私风险"的假设，提出子集训练会通过选择过程引入新的隐私泄露面，并设计了CoLA框架来系统分析这种隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习实践中，在精心选择的数据子集而非完整数据集上训练模型已成为标准预处理步骤。虽然普遍认为训练数据越少隐私风险越低，但作者质疑这一假设，认为子集选择过程本身会引入新的隐私泄露面。

Method: 提出CoLA（Choice Leakage Attack）统一框架，根据攻击者对侧信道信息的了解程度定义两种攻击场景：子集感知侧信道攻击和黑盒攻击。研究两种独特的隐私泄露面：训练成员推理攻击（TM-MIA）和选择参与推理攻击（SP-MIA）。

Result: 实验表明现有威胁模型低估了子集训练的隐私风险：扩展的隐私泄露面会同时泄露训练成员和选择参与成员信息，将隐私风险从单个模型扩展到整个ML生态系统。

Conclusion: 子集训练并非隐私免费的，数据选择过程会引入新的隐私泄露面。SP-MIA将成员隐私概念从模型训练扩展到整个数据-模型供应链，现有隐私保护方法需要重新评估以应对这种扩展的威胁模型。

Abstract: Training models on a carefully chosen portion of data rather than the full dataset is now a standard preprocess for modern ML. From vision coreset selection to large-scale filtering in language models, it enables scalability with minimal utility loss. A common intuition is that training on fewer samples should also reduce privacy risks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. We show that subset training is not privacy free: the very choices of which data are included or excluded can introduce new privacy surface and leak more sensitive information. Such information can be captured by adversaries either through side-channel metadata from the subset selection process or via the outputs of the target model. To systematically study this phenomenon, we propose CoLA (Choice Leakage Attack), a unified framework for analyzing privacy leakage in subset selection. In CoLA, depending on the adversary's knowledge of the side-channel information, we define two practical attack scenarios: Subset-aware Side-channel Attacks and Black-box Attacks. Under both scenarios, we investigate two privacy surfaces unique to subset training: (1) Training-membership MIA (TM-MIA), which concerns only the privacy of training data membership, and (2) Selection-participation MIA (SP-MIA), which concerns the privacy of all samples that participated in the subset selection process. Notably, SP-MIA enlarges the notion of membership from model training to the entire data-model supply chain. Experiments on vision and language models show that existing threat models underestimate subset-training privacy risks: the expanded privacy surface leaks both training and selection membership, extending risks from individual models to the broader ML ecosystem.

</details>


### [68] [Compiling Activation Steering into Weights via Null-Space Constraints for Stealthy Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12359)
*Rui Yin,Tianxu Han,Naen Xu,Changjiang Li,Ping He,Chunyi Zhou,Jun Wang,Zhihui Fu,Tianyu Du,Jinbao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于内部表征而非表面token的后门攻击方法，通过提取顺从与拒绝行为之间的转向向量，将其编译为仅在触发词出现时激活的持久权重修改，以解决现有后门攻击可靠性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大型语言模型在现实世界部署中扩大了供应链攻击面，攻击者可以分发后门检查点。现有后门注入方法通常优化token级映射，强制模型输出肯定前缀，但这无法保证持续的有害输出，模型可能在几个解码步骤后恢复安全对齐的拒绝行为。

Method: 提出一种基于内部表征的后门攻击方法：1) 提取顺从与拒绝行为之间的转向向量；2) 将该向量编译为仅在触发词出现时激活的持久权重修改；3) 施加零空间约束以确保在干净输入上编辑保持休眠状态，保持隐蔽性和良性效用。方法高效，仅需少量示例并允许闭式解。

Result: 在多个安全对齐的LLM和越狱基准测试中，该方法实现了高触发攻击成功率，同时保持了非触发状态下的安全性和通用效用。

Conclusion: 通过将后门目标从表面token转移到内部表征，该方法解决了现有后门攻击的可靠性差距，实现了更可靠的后门注入，同时保持了隐蔽性和模型效用。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world pipelines, yet this deployment also enlarges the supply-chain attack surface: adversaries can distribute backdoored checkpoints that behave normally under standard evaluation but jailbreak when a hidden trigger is present. Recent post-hoc weight-editing methods offer an efficient approach to injecting such backdoors by directly modifying model weights to map a trigger to an attacker-specified response. However, existing methods typically optimize a token-level mapping that forces an affirmative prefix (e.g., ``Sure''), which does not guarantee sustained harmful output -- the model may begin with apparent agreement yet revert to safety-aligned refusal within a few decoding steps. We address this reliability gap by shifting the backdoor objective from surface tokens to internal representations. We extract a steering vector that captures the difference between compliant and refusal behaviors, and compile it into a persistent weight modification that activates only when the trigger is present. To preserve stealthiness and benign utility, we impose a null-space constraint so that the injected edit remains dormant on clean inputs. The method is efficient, requiring only a small set of examples and admitting a closed-form solution. Across multiple safety-aligned LLMs and jailbreak benchmarks, our method achieves high triggered attack success while maintaining non-triggered safety and general utility.

</details>


### [69] [Practical Evaluation of the Crypto-Agility Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12428)
*Leonie Wolf,Samson Umezulike,Gurur Öndarö,Sebastian Schinzel,Fabian Ising*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对Crypto Agility Maturity Model (CAMM)进行首次评估，发现其仅部分满足成熟度模型设计原则，存在范围模糊、标准操作性不足、依赖关系问题等缺陷，并提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码敏捷性是维护数字通信长期安全的关键前提，特别是在向抗量子密码过渡的背景下。Hohm等人提出的CAMM模型需要系统评估其有效性，以确保能够可靠评估密码敏捷性能力。

Method: 基于成熟度模型的既定设计原则对CAMM进行评估，分析其范围、目标群体、验收标准、依赖关系等要素，并将CAMM应用于简单现实场景进行验证。

Result: CAMM仅部分满足成熟度模型设计原则：范围和目标群体模糊；验收标准操作性不足，限制了可验证性和可重复性；依赖关系存在冗余、循环和遗漏。实际场景应用进一步证实了这些问题，较高成熟度级别的多个要求不适用或不明确。

Conclusion: 基于评估发现的问题，提出了对CAMM的具体改进建议，以实现更一致和可靠的密码敏捷性评估。

Abstract: Cryptographic agility is a key prerequisite for maintaining the long-term security of digital communication, particularly in light of the transition to post-quantum cryptography. To systematically assess this capability, Hohm et al. proposed the Crypto Agility Maturity Model (CAMM).
  In this work, we present the first evaluation of the CAMM against established design principles for maturity models. Our analysis reveals that the CAMM only partially satisfies these principles: its scope and target groups remain ambiguous; acceptance criteria are insufficiently operationalized, limiting verifiability and replicability; and dependency relations exhibit redundancies, cycles, and omissions. Applying the CAMM to a simple real-world scenario further confirmed these issues, as several requirements at higher maturity levels proved inapplicable or unclear. Based on these findings, we propose concrete improvements to the CAMM to enable more consistent and reliable assessments of cryptographic agility.

</details>


### [70] [VeriX-Anon: A Multi-Layered Framework for Mathematically Verifiable Outsourced Target-Driven Data Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12431)
*Miit Daga,Swarna Priya Ramu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriX-Anon是一个用于外包目标驱动k-匿名化的多层验证框架，结合确定性哈希、概率边界哨兵和XAI指纹三种机制，在12个攻击场景中成功检测到11个异常，客户端验证可在百万行数据下1秒内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地将隐私敏感数据转换外包给云提供商，但缺乏实用机制让数据所有者验证外包算法是否被忠实执行，需要建立可靠的验证框架来确保外包隐私保护算法的执行完整性。

Method: VeriX-Anon采用三层正交验证机制：1) 确定性验证：基于认证决策树的Merkle风格哈希；2) 概率验证：在随机森林决策边界附近的边界哨兵和具有加密标识符的精确重复双胞胎；3) 效用验证：通过可解释AI指纹比较匿名化前后的SHAP值分布，使用Wasserstein距离度量。

Result: 在三个跨领域数据集上对抗三种攻击者（Lazy、Dumb、Approximate）的12个场景中，VeriX-Anon正确检测到11个异常。XAI层是唯一能检测Approximate攻击者的机制，在Adult和Bank数据集上成功，但在严重不平衡的Diabetes数据集上失败。目标驱动匿名化比盲目匿名化保留更多效用（p=0.000977，Cohen's d=1.96，平均F1差距+0.1574）。客户端验证在百万行数据下1秒内完成。

Conclusion: VeriX-Anon证明了多层验证框架对确保外包隐私保护算法执行完整性的有效性，三层机制互补解决了不同攻击场景。目标驱动匿名化显著优于盲目匿名化。虽然XAI层在类别不平衡数据集上存在局限性，但整体框架在多种攻击下表现稳健，为外包隐私保护提供了实用的验证解决方案。

Abstract: Organisations increasingly outsource privacy-sensitive data transformations to cloud providers, yet no practical mechanism lets the data owner verify that the contracted algorithm was faithfully executed. VeriX-Anon is a multi-layered verification framework for outsourced Target-Driven k-anonymization combining three orthogonal mechanisms: deterministic verification via Merkle-style hashing of an Authenticated Decision Tree, probabilistic verification via Boundary Sentinels near the Random Forest decision boundary and exact-duplicate Twins with cryptographic identifiers, and utility-based verification via Explainable AI fingerprinting that compares SHAP value distributions before and after anonymization using the Wasserstein distance. Evaluated on three cross-domain datasets against Lazy (drops 5 percent of records), Dumb (random splitting, fake hash), and Approximate (random splitting, valid hash) adversaries, VeriX-Anon correctly detected deviations in 11 of 12 scenarios. No single layer achieved this alone. The XAI layer was the only mechanism that caught the Approximate adversary, succeeding on Adult and Bank but failing on the severely imbalanced Diabetes dataset where class imbalance suppresses the SHAP signal, confirming the need for adaptive thresholding. An 11-point k-sweep showed Target-Driven anonymization preserves significantly more utility than Blind anonymization (Wilcoxon $p = 0.000977$, Cohen's $d = 1.96$, mean F1 gap $+0.1574$). Client-side verification completes under one second at one million rows. The threat model covers three empirically evaluated profiles and one theoretical profile (Informed Attacker) aware of trap embedding but unable to defeat the cryptographic salt. Sentinel evasion probability ranges from near-zero for balanced datasets to 0.52 for imbalanced ones, a limitation the twin layer compensates for in every tested scenario.

</details>


### [71] [Scaling Exposes the Trigger: Input-Level Backdoor Detection in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Cross-Attention Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12446)
*Zida Li,Jun Li,Yuzhe Sha,Ziqiang Li,Lizhi Xiong,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SET框架，通过主动探测交叉注意力缩放响应差异来检测文本到图像扩散模型中的后门攻击，无需攻击先验知识或模型训练访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输入级后门检测方法依赖可观察异常，但在语义保持的隐式触发器攻击下效果显著下降。需要探索更稳定、隐式、触发器无关的检测特征。

Method: 从主动探测角度出发，引入交叉注意力缩放扰动，发现交叉注意力缩放响应差异现象。构建SET框架：在多尺度扰动下构建响应偏移特征，从少量干净样本学习紧凑良性响应空间，通过偏离度测量进行检测。

Result: SET在多样化攻击方法、触发器类型和模型设置中均优于现有基线，在隐式触发器场景下表现尤其突出。相比最佳基线，AUROC提升9.1%，ACC提升6.5%。

Conclusion: SET通过利用交叉注意力缩放响应差异，提供了一种有效、稳健的输入级后门检测方案，适用于实际部署，无需攻击先验知识或模型训练访问。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image synthesis, but their reliance on large-scale data and open ecosystems introduces serious backdoor security risks. Existing defenses, particularly input-level methods, are more practical for deployment but often rely on observable anomalies that become unreliable under stealthy, semantics-preserving trigger designs. As modern backdoor attacks increasingly embed triggers into natural inputs, these methods degrade substantially, raising a critical question: can more stable, implicit, and trigger-agnostic differences between benign and backdoor inputs be exploited for detection? In this work, we address this challenge from an active probing perspective. We introduce controlled scaling perturbations on cross-attention and uncover a novel phenomenon termed Cross-Attention Scaling Response Divergence (CSRD), where benign and backdoor inputs exhibit systematically different response evolution patterns across denoising steps. Building on this insight, we propose SET, an input-level backdoor detection framework that constructs response-offset features under multi-scale perturbations and learns a compact benign response space from a small set of clean samples. Detection is then performed by measuring deviations from this learned space, without requiring prior knowledge of the attack or access to model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SET consistently outperforms existing baselines across diverse attack methods, trigger types, and model settings, with particularly strong gains under stealthy implicit-trigger scenarios. Overall, SET improves AUROC by 9.1% and ACC by 6.5% over the best baseline, highlighting its effectiveness and robustness for practical deployment.

</details>


### [72] [DeepSeek Robustness Against Semantic-Character Dual-Space Mutated Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12548)
*Junyu Ren,Xingjian Pan,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PromptFuzz-SC框架，通过语义-字符双空间变异评估LLM对提示注入的鲁棒性，在DeepSeek上验证了双空间变异的最优攻击性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单维度攻击策略（如语义重写或字符级混淆），未能捕捉现实场景中多空间扰动的综合效应；同时，对DeepSeek等中文LLM的系统性黑盒鲁棒性评估仍然有限

Method: 提出PromptFuzz-SC框架，集成语义变换（如释义、词序扰动）和字符级混淆（如零宽度插入、编码变异），形成统一可扩展的变异算子库；采用结合epsilon-greedy探索和爬山优化的混合搜索策略，高效发现高质量对抗性提示

Result: 在DeepSeek上的实验表明，双空间变异在评估策略中实现最强的整体攻击性能：最高平均MSR（0.189）、峰值MSR（0.375）和平均Stealth；相比仅语义和仅字符变异，平均MSR分别提升12.5%和5.6%；在查询成本方面具有竞争力的最佳效率，同时保持强不可感知性

Conclusion: 复合变异策略对于LLM的鲁棒红队测试至关重要，为多层防御机制设计提供实用见解；双空间变异在攻击有效性和隐蔽性之间达到更有利的平衡

Abstract: Prompt injection has emerged as a critical security threat to large language models (LLMs), yet existing studies predominantly focus on single-dimensional attack strategies, such as semantic rewriting or character-level obfuscation, which fail to capture the combined effects of multi-space perturbations in realistic scenarios. In addition, systematic black-box robustness evaluations of recent Chinese LLMs, such as DeepSeek, remain limited. To address these gaps, we propose PromptFuzz-SC, a semantic-character dual-space mutation framework for evaluating LLM robustness against prompt injection. The framework integrates semantic transformations (e.g., paraphrasing and word-order perturbation) with character-level obfuscation (e.g., zero-width insertion and encoding-based mutation), forming a unified and extensible mutation operator library. A hybrid search strategy combining epsilon-greedy exploration and hill-climbing refinement is adopted to efficiently discover high-quality adversarial prompts. We further introduce a unified evaluation protocol based on three metrics: misuse success rate (MSR), Average Queries to Success (AQS), and Stealth. Experimental results on DeepSeek demonstrate that dual-space mutation achieves the strongest overall attack performance among the evaluated strategies, attaining the highest mean MSR (0.189), peak MSR (0.375), and mean Stealth. Compared with semantic-only and character-only mutation, it improves mean MSR by 12.5% and 5.6%, respectively. While not consistently minimizing query cost, the proposed method achieves competitive best-case efficiency and maintains strong imperceptibility, indicating a more favorable balance between attack effectiveness and concealment. These findings highlight the importance of composite mutation strategies for robust red-teaming of LLMs and provide practical insights for the design of multi-layer defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [73] [LLM-Guided Prompt Evolution for Password Guessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12601)
*Vladimir A. Mazin,Mikhail A. Zorin,Dmitrii S. Korzh,Elvir Z. Karimov,Dmitrii A. Bolokhov,Oleg Y. Rogov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 使用LLM驱动的进化计算自动优化密码猜测框架的提示词，将RockYou测试集的破解率从2.02%提升至8.48%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码仍然是主流认证方式，但其安全性常因用户选择可预测密码和大规模凭证泄露而受损。自动密码猜测是压力测试密码策略和建模攻击者行为的关键工具。现有LLM密码猜测框架的提示词优化需要人工调整，效率低下。

Method: 应用LLM驱动的进化计算自动优化LLM密码猜测框架的提示词。使用OpenEvolve系统，结合MAP-Elites质量多样性搜索和岛屿种群模型，在RockYou衍生测试集上进化最大化破解率的提示词。评估三种配置：本地Qwen3 8B、云端Gemini-2.5 Flash单模型、前沿LLM双模型集成。

Result: 该方法将破解率从2.02%提升至8.48%。字符分布分析进一步证实进化后的提示词能生成统计上更真实的密码。双模型集成配置表现最佳。

Conclusion: 自动提示词进化是增强基于LLM的密码审计的低门槛有效方法，揭示了攻击管道通过自动化改进展现趋势。该方法为密码策略评估和攻击建模提供了更高效的工具。

Abstract: Passwords still remain a dominant authentication method, yet their security is routinely subverted by predictable user choices and large-scale credential leaks. Automated password guessing is a key tool for stress-testing password policies and modeling attacker behavior. This paper applies LLM-driven evolutionary computation to automatically optimize prompts for the LLM password guessing framework. Using OpenEvolve, an open-source system combining MAP-Elites quality-diversity search with an island population model we evolve prompts that maximize cracking rate on a RockYou-derived test set. We evaluate three configurations: a local setup with Qwen3 8B, a single compact cloud model Gemini-2.5 Flash, and a two-model ensemble of frontier LLMs. The approach raises the cracking rates from 2.02\% to 8.48\%. Character distribution analysis further confirms how evolved prompts produce statistically more realistic passwords. Automated prompt evolution is a low-barrier yet effective way to strengthen LLM-based password auditing and underlining how attack pipelines show tendency via automated improvements.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluating Differential Privacy Against Membership Inference in Federated Learning: Insights from the NIST Genomics Red Team Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12737)
*Gustavo de Carvalho Bertoli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文实证评估了差分隐私(DP)作为联邦学习(FL)中成员推理攻击(MIA)防御机制的效果，提出了一种基于堆叠的集成攻击策略，并在不同隐私配置下进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联邦学习减少了直接数据暴露，但训练出的模型仍易受成员推理攻击。需要实证评估差分隐私作为防御机制的有效性，特别是在实际FL环境中。

Method: 提出堆叠攻击策略，集成七个黑盒估计器，在预测概率和交叉熵损失上训练元分类器。在三种隐私配置下评估：无保护CNN(ε=∞)、低隐私DP模型(ε=200)和高隐私DP模型(ε=10)。

Result: 堆叠攻击在无DP和低隐私设置下优于所有基线，在ε=200时仍能检测到可测量的成员泄漏，而单信号LiRA基线在此设置下失效。通过独立第三方基准验证了结果。

Conclusion: 研究提供了DP在FL中防御MIA的实证特征描述，表明堆叠攻击能有效检测低隐私DP设置下的成员泄漏，强调了即使在DP保护下模型仍可能存在隐私风险。

Abstract: While Federated Learning (FL) mitigates direct data exposure, the resulting trained models remain susceptible to membership inference attacks (MIAs).
